       "main_text_excerpt": "Placement test\nFind your Norwegian level\nNot sure which Norwegian course is right for you? Take one of our free placement tests to get an indication of your current level and which course you could continue with.\nIf you have never studied Norwegian before, you do not need to take a placement test. You can start with A1.1.\nChoose your placement test\nChoose the level you have already studied. The test helps us assess whether you are ready to continue to the next course.\nIf you are unsure which level you have completed, you can take more than one test.\nA1\nA2\nYou can take more than one test\nYou do not need to know your exact level before you start. If you are unsure, you can take several different placement tests. This can give us a better overview of your reading and writing skills.\nFor example, if you are unsure whether your level is around A2 or B1, you can complete both relevant tests and tell us which tests you have taken when you contact us.\nThere is normally no reason to take the same test several times. If the same test is completed repeatedly, we will use your first attempt when assessing your level.\nWhat does the placement test measure?\nThe free online placement tests focus on your reading and writing skills. They do not assess all four language skills.\nIncluded in the online test\n- ✓ Reading\n- ✓ Writing\nNot included in the online test\n- – Speaking\n- – Listening\nYour result therefore gives us an indication of your level, rather than a complete assessment of your Norwegian across all four skills.\nFull level assessment with a teacher\nIf you are unsure of your level or want all four language skills to be assessed, you can book an individual assessment with one of our teachers.\nWho is the assessment for?\nThe assessment is particularly useful if your language skills are uneven. For example, you may speak Norwegian confidently but have less experience with grammar and writing, or your written Norwegian may be stronger than your spoken Norwegian.\nWhat happens after the assessment?\nThe teacher assesses your Norwegian level based on all four language skills. Reception will then follow up with a recommendation for the course level that is likely to suit you best.\nYou will also receive a simple email confirming the teacher’s assessment of your Norwegian level.\nNeed a more detailed written assessment?\nIf you would like a more detailed evaluation of your performance in reading, writing, listening and speaking, you can also order a written assessment report.\nThe report costs NOK 1,198.\nHow the tests work\nA1–B1 placement tests\nThese tests mainly consist of questions where you choose the correct answer. They assess your understanding of written Norwegian and your knowledge of language structures at the relevant level.\nB2 placement test\nThe B2 test includes a longer writing task. Because your answer needs to be assessed by a teacher, it may take some time before you receive feedback on your result.\nWhat happens after the test?\nYour test score is only one part of deciding which course is right for you. Contact Alfaskolen after you have completed the test, and we can help you interpret your result.\nComplete the test\nTake the test that corresponds to the level you have already studied. You may take more than one different test if you are unsure.\nContact Alfaskolen\nTell us which placement test or tests you completed so that we can review your result.\nHave you completed the B2 writing test?\nThe B2 writing task must be assessed manually by one of our teachers. Please contact reception after submitting your answer and let us know that you have completed the B2 test.\nYou can normally expect to receive your assessment within five business days.\nContact Alfaskolen\nGet an accurate result\nThe purpose of a placement test is not to achieve the highest possible score. The purpose is to find the course that matches your actual Norwegian level.\nComplete the test independently and without using AI tools, translation services, dictionaries or help from another person.\nUsing these tools may give you a higher score, but it can also result in a recommendation for a course that is too difficult for your current level.\nDo your best, but do not worry if you do not know every answer. That is exactly what the placement test is designed to identify.\nPlacement test FAQ\nWhich placement test should I take?\nChoose the test for the level you have already studied. For example, if you have completed A2 and are considering B1, take the A2 placement test.\nI have never studied Norwegian. Do I need a placement test?\nNo. If you are a complete beginner and have not studied Norwegian before, you can normally start directly with A1.1.\nCan I take more than one placement test?\nYes. If you are unsure which level best reflects your current Norwegian, you may take several different placement tests. This can give us more information when recommending a course.\nCan I take the same test more than once?\nYou can, but there is normally no benefit in repeating the same te",
